# X01 — Controller/menu exhaustion matrix (protocol E.4 / section 8)

### X01-001 — what this segment is
- expected: a note event carries this observation into events.jsonl
- actual: Protocol E.4, the controller/menu exhaustion matrix. Recorded per cell: {focus_before, input_raw, resolved_action, world_side_effect, ui_side_effect, focus_after, recoverable}. The operator records what happens; diagnoses are Phase B's.
- events: t=0.00
- verdict: PASS

### X01-002 — free_build stays OFF
- expected: a note event carries this observation into events.jsonl
- actual: free_build is OFF for this segment and every other segment except X02's single labelled banner check. Building and crafting are resource-bound (protocol E.3 preamble).
- events: t=0.00
- verdict: PASS

### X01-003 — AMBIGUITY: the pause shell as a matrix row
- expected: a note event carries this observation into events.jsonl
- actual: E.4 lists 'pause shell + each of the seven tabs' as separate contexts. game_menu.gd opens onto a tab, so the shell with no tab chosen is not a state a player can reach and gate_f_probe.gd will report the shell row as menu_backpack, the same string the Satchel row reports. Transcribed as its own row anyway -- the cells are probed on the opening frame, before any tab navigation -- and the duplicate context string is recorded here rather than silently collapsed.
- events: t=0.00
- verdict: PASS

### X01-004 — Part A: from S03-exit
- expected: a note event carries this observation into events.jsonl
- actual: E.4's village-reachable contexts, from the journey's S03 exit save: all systems unlocked in the village.
- events: t=0.00
- verdict: PASS

### X01-005 — empty the live save directory
- expected: every slot file is gone, so the seeded slot 4 is the only thing the title's Load list can offer and the auto-focus cannot land on an autosave
- actual: wiped 2 files from /root/.local/share/godot/app_userdata/Tetherbound/saves (kept slots [])
- events: t=0.00
- verdict: PASS

### X01-006 — seed slot 4 from S03-exit
- expected: the journey's S03-exit slot file is back in user://, so the title's Load Game finds it
- actual: seeded slot 4 from ralph/reports/gate-f-run-20260827T025303Z/S03/saves/S03-exit.json (1415098 bytes)
- events: t=0.01
- verdict: PASS

- events: t=63.78

### X01-733 — AMBIGUITY: a catch throw against a trainer-owned creature

### X01-762 — VOCABULARY GAP: no same-frame probe at a combat edge
- expected: a note event carries this observation into events.jsonl
- actual: press_multi is the same-frame primitive and it needs to name the frame. A fight starts and ends on a frame the harness does not choose -- the trainer flow and the opponent's defeat pick it -- so the world<->combat boundary can be given next-frame probes (press the shared button on the frame after the edge) but not same-frame ones. Reported before the freeze rather than discovered mid-run.
- events: t=580.55
- verdict: PASS

### X01-1078 — VOCABULARY GAP: the keyboard half of a dual-bound action cannot be selected
- expected: a note event carries this observation into events.jsonl
- actual: operator_harness.gd::_physical_binding prefers a joypad event, then a key, then a mouse button, and SEGMENT_SCHEMA.md's `press` has no `device` argument. So W/A/S/D (move_*), E (interact), I (inventory), M (map), Esc (game_menu/menu_cancel) and Tab (menu_tab_right) are all ALSO bound to the pad and will be injected as pad events, not as keys. Only the keyboard-only and mouse-only actions -- B (build_open), L (torch_toggle), F (combat_throw), Esc-as-combat_run, P (torch_place), LMB (use_tool) -- actually reach the KBM path through this vocabulary. Transcribed as written; the gap is reported before the freeze because a `device` argument is a harness change, not an operator change.
- events: t=763.54
- verdict: PASS
